The file says what the agent may do, how much it may spend, where it may write, where it runs and who it escalates to. Nothing about an agent exists only in a console, a repository variable or a database row without its manifest saying so. The registry in D1 (#8080) and the runtime checks (#8070 ([AxalNetwork/StudioOS](https://github.com/AxalNetwork/StudioOS/blob/HEAD/agents/README.md))
- **README**: E3 of #3742 (#3847). The agent engine's runtime: what admits an agent's run, runs its steps, waits for a person's decision and stops when told to. It never decides anything itself: a run's output is always a proposal in #3727's approvals queue (`cloudflare-worker/src/services/agents/queue.ts`), and a person decides it. Everything here is off unless `AGENT_ENGINE` is not `off` and `TEAM_AGENTS_ENAB ([AxalNetwork/StudioOS](https://github.com/AxalNetwork/StudioOS/blob/HEAD/cloudflare-worker/src/agents/README.md))
